Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The multiple operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 264. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 355 with 2 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_264(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 264. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^355(S, F) is trivial. QED.
